# Korean Core 2–5 and AION 2 verification table (M4-1, M4-1b)

Every entry links to a National Institute of Korean Language learner's dictionary (KRDict) search.
**Status:** the entries were authored from the author's own knowledge and **have not yet been checked against the dictionary**.
Before shipping, a web-capable agent or the owner should tick ✓ in the *Checked* column after confirming the meaning, the Revised Romanization, and (for verbs/adjectives) the irregular class against the linked entry.
Conjugation output for every verb/adjective was generated by `Conjugator` and reviewed by hand (see PLAN §17 for the known gap: 춤추다 → 안 춤춰요).
